- **fiscal-regime-ifu-auto-entrepreneur**: Gérer le régime de l'impôt forfaitaire unique (IFU) et le statut d'auto-entrepreneur en Algérie dans un logiciel : seuils, taux, minimum de perception, G12/G12 bis, échéancier, dépassement de seuil. À utiliser pour un module de petits commerçants et artisans. ([hamzazerouala/jazair-claude](https://github.com/hamzazerouala/jazair-claude/tree/HEAD/plugins/jazair/skills/fiscal-regime-ifu-auto-entrepreneur))
- **fiscal-rendimientos-p2p**: Calcula el tratamiento fiscal de intereses cobrados por una persona física en plataformas de crowdfunding de deuda (IFC) - retención de ISR que la IFC hace en sustitución del solicitante (20% sobre intereses nominales, regla 3.16.13 RMF desde oct-2024), interés real acumulable (Cap. VI LISR), acreditamiento en la declaración anual con el CFDI de retenciones y tratamiento de créditos incumplidos. Usar cuando el usuario diga fiscal rendimientos p2p, fiscal_rendimientos_p2p, impuestos crowdfunding, ISR de intereses de préstamos P2P, cómo declaro mis intereses de crowdfunding, ayuda con fiscal re… ([elimorals/Skills_MX](https://github.com/elimorals/Skills_MX/tree/HEAD/crowdfunding-itf-mx/skills/fiscal-rendimientos-p2p) · ★29)
- **fiscal-retenue-a-la-source**: Implémenter les retenues à la source algériennes (IRG et IBS) dans un logiciel de paiement fournisseur ou de comptabilité : taux par nature de revenu, non-résidents, conventions fiscales, brut/net, redevances logiciels, échéances de versement. Script testé. ([hamzazerouala/jazair-claude](https://github.com/hamzazerouala/jazair-claude/tree/HEAD/plugins/jazair/skills/fiscal-retenue-a-la-source))
- **fiscal-review**: Revisar um diff ou plano contra as regras fiscais do freecorpsNFE: valores decimais, chave de acesso, CNPJ e CPF, datas de Brasília, XML original e histórico, eventos, ambientes e gates, emissão NFS-e, captura e guarda. Use quando a mudança toca NF-e, NFS-e, ABRASF, valores ou dados fiscais. ([freecorps/freecorpsNFE](https://github.com/freecorps/freecorpsNFE/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/fiscal-review) · 2 copies)
- **fiscal-sponsorship-agreement**: Drafts a Fiscal Sponsorship Agreement between a 501(c)(3) sponsor and an unincorporated or non-exempt project. Enforces IRS compliance via sponsor control, variance power, separate accounting, and donor substantiation under IRC § 170. Use when forming fiscal sponsorship arrangements, onboarding sponsored projects, or drafting charitable project funding agreements. ([CaseMark/skills](https://github.com/CaseMark/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/legal/fiscal-sponsorship-agreement) · ★54 · 4 copies)
- **fiscal-taxes-locales-tap**: Gérer dans un logiciel algérien la fin de la TAP, la taxe locale de solidarité (transport par canalisation, mines), la taxe de formation et d'apprentissage et la répartition aux collectivités. Évite d'appliquer une TAP abrogée. Script de calcul testé. ([hamzazerouala/jazair-claude](https://github.com/hamzazerouala/jazair-claude/tree/HEAD/plugins/jazair/skills/fiscal-taxes-locales-tap))
- **fiscal-tva**: Implémenter la TVA algérienne dans un logiciel de facturation ou de caisse : taux 19 % et 9 %, calcul HT/TVA/TTC, ventilation par taux, arrondis, cas des clients IFU et obligations logiciel 2027. À utiliser pour tout calcul de TVA ou module de facturation. ([hamzazerouala/jazair-claude](https://github.com/hamzazerouala/jazair-claude/tree/HEAD/plugins/jazair/skills/fiscal-tva))
- **fiscal-year-end-report**: Drafts the narrative section of a fiscal year-end financial report — variance analysis, key revenue and expense drivers, unrestricted vs. restricted fund performance, endowment performance summary, and forward outlook. Multi-version framing for GASB (public institutions) or FASB ASC 958 (private not-for-profits). Use when the controller, CFO, or director of accounting needs the prose section that accompanies the audited financial statements or the management discussion and analysis (MD&A). ([whitegloveai/claude-skills-highered](https://github.com/whitegloveai/claude-skills-highered/tree/HEAD/plugins/finance-business/skills/fiscal-year-end-report))
- **fiscalapi-carta-porte**: Timbra con Fiscalapi un CFDI/Factura de carta porte (complemento Carta Porte 3.1, autotransporte), de traslado (tipo T) o de ingreso por el flete (tipo I), con moneda, receptor y conceptos según el tipo, ubicaciones con domicilio y distancias, mercancías, autotransporte y figuras de transporte, sin los rechazos CP más comunes. Úsala cuando vayas a timbrar una carta porte o a amparar con CFDI el transporte de mercancías por carretera. No la uses para diagnosticar un error CP que ya devolvió la API (usa `fiscalapi-diagnostico`). ([FiscalAPI/fiscalapi-ai](https://github.com/FiscalAPI/fiscalapi-ai/tree/HEAD/skills/fiscalapi-carta-porte) · ★2)
- **fiscalapi-ciclo-de-vida**: Gestiona un CFDI de Fiscalapi después de timbrarlo. Cubre la cancelación ante el SAT (motivos 01-04, UUID sustituto y aceptación del receptor), el estatus ante el SAT, la descarga del XML y el PDF, el envío por correo y los webhooks (registro, eventos, firma, reintentos y pruebas en local con `fiscalapi listen` y `fiscalapi trigger`). Úsala cuando vayas a cancelar, consultar, descargar o enviar una factura ya emitida, o a recibir sus eventos en tu sistema. No la uses para emitir (usa `fiscalapi-ingreso-egreso` o `fiscalapi-pagos`) ni para revisar una integración completa (usa el comando `revi… ([FiscalAPI/fiscalapi-ai](https://github.com/FiscalAPI/fiscalapi-ai/tree/HEAD/skills/fiscalapi-ciclo-de-vida) · ★2)
- **fiscalapi-comercio-exterior**: Timbra con Fiscalapi un CFDI/Factura de comercio exterior (complemento Comercio Exterior 2.0, exportación definitiva) con exportación 02, moneda y tipo de cambio USD del DOF, receptor extranjero, clave de pedimento A1, Incoterm, domicilios y mercancías con fracción arancelaria, unidad de aduana y valor en dólares por concepto, sin los rechazos CCE más comunes. Úsala cuando vayas a facturar una venta al extranjero o una exportación de mercancías. No la uses para diagnosticar un error CCE que ya devolvió la API (usa `fiscalapi-diagnostico`). ([FiscalAPI/fiscalapi-ai](https://github.com/FiscalAPI/fiscalapi-ai/tree/HEAD/skills/fiscalapi-comercio-exterior) · ★2)
- **fiscalapi-diagnostico**: Diagnostica una respuesta de error de la API de Fiscalapi (CFDI 4.0) y propone la corrección. Distingue una validación de Fiscalapi (400 con propertyName), un rechazo del PAC o del SAT (código como CFDI40147, CRP20101, NOM5 o CP101 en message), un conflicto o rechazo de la llave de idempotencia (409, 422), un error de credenciales, saldo o límite (401, 403, 429) y un error del servidor (5xx), y lleva a la página de docs.fiscalapi.com con la causa. Úsala cuando una llamada a Fiscalapi falla al timbrar, cancelar o consultar. No la uses para diseñar una factura nueva ni para errores de tu propio… ([FiscalAPI/fiscalapi-ai](https://github.com/FiscalAPI/fiscalapi-ai/tree/HEAD/skills/fiscalapi-diagnostico) · ★2)
- **fiscalapi-ingreso-egreso**: Timbra con Fiscalapi CFDI/Factura de ingreso, CFDI/Factura de egreso (nota de crédito) y CFDI/Factura global sin rechazos del SAT. Decide régimen fiscal y uso CFDI del receptor, objeto de impuesto e impuestos del concepto, método y forma de pago, la relación de la nota de crédito y la información global de público en general, y busca las claves del SAT con la API de catálogos. Úsala cuando vayas a emitir o corregir una factura de ingreso, egreso o global. No la uses para el complemento de pago (usa `fiscalapi-pagos`) ni para un error que ya devolvió la API (usa `fiscalapi-diagnostico`). ([FiscalAPI/fiscalapi-ai](https://github.com/FiscalAPI/fiscalapi-ai/tree/HEAD/skills/fiscalapi-ingreso-egreso) · ★2)
- **fiscalapi-integracion**: Integra Fiscalapi (facturación electrónica CFDI 4.0 en México) en un ERP, POS o SaaS. Elige el SDK según el lenguaje del proyecto, configura el ambiente de pruebas y los headers X-API-KEY, X-TENANT-KEY y X-TIME-ZONE, decide entre el modo por valores y por referencias, reintenta sin duplicar con Idempotency-Key, y lleva a la sección exacta de docs.fiscalapi.com y a la skill de cada tipo de factura. Úsala cuando te pidan integrar Fiscalapi, timbrar o emitir facturas CFDI desde código, reintentar una emisión, o empezar con Fiscalapi desde cero. No la uses para diagnosticar un error que ya devolv… ([FiscalAPI/fiscalapi-ai](https://github.com/FiscalAPI/fiscalapi-ai/tree/HEAD/skills/fiscalapi-integracion) · ★2)
- **fiscalapi-nomina**: Timbra con Fiscalapi un CFDI/Factura de nómina (tipo N, complemento de nómina 1.2), ordinaria o extraordinaria, con los datos del empleado y del empleador por referencias o por valores, el periodo, las percepciones, las deducciones, los otros pagos con el subsidio para el empleo y las incapacidades, sin los rechazos NOM más comunes. Úsala cuando vayas a timbrar recibos de nómina o a registrar los datos laborales de empleados y empleadores. No la uses para diagnosticar un error NOM que ya devolvió la API (usa `fiscalapi-diagnostico`). ([FiscalAPI/fiscalapi-ai](https://github.com/FiscalAPI/fiscalapi-ai/tree/HEAD/skills/fiscalapi-nomina) · ★2)
- **fiscalapi-pagos**: Timbra con Fiscalapi el CFDI/Factura de pago (complemento de recepción de pagos 2.0, REP, tipo P) de una factura PPD, en una o varias parcialidades. Decide si hace falta (PUE o PPD), arma `complement.payment` con saldo anterior, importe pagado y saldo insoluto, el número de parcialidad, el tipo de cambio y la equivalencia, y los impuestos del documento pagado. Úsala cuando el receptor pague una factura con método PPD o te pidan un REP. No la uses para la factura original (usa `fiscalapi-ingreso-egreso`) ni para un error CRP que ya devolvió la API (usa `fiscalapi-diagnostico`). ([FiscalAPI/fiscalapi-ai](https://github.com/FiscalAPI/fiscalapi-ai/tree/HEAD/skills/fiscalapi-pagos) · ★2)

- **fisher-exact-contingency-analysis**: Use when both an OMARU MWAS pathway enrichment file (sumstats_*_annot.txt) and a PASCAL-derived GWAS pathway enrichment file (&lt;Phenotype&gt;_GWAS_&lt;Pathway_reference&gt;.txt) exist for the same phenotype and you must quantify their pathway-level overlap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/fisher-exact-contingency-analysis))
- **fisher-exact-contingency-table-implementation**: Use when performing metabolomics ORA and you need per-pathway p-values from a right-tailed Fisher's exact test (hypergeometric) on the pathway collection, metabolites-of-interest list, and background compound set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fisher-exact-contingency-table-implementation))
- **fisher-exact-contingency-table-testing**: Use when two WGCNA/PyWGCNA co-expression networks from different datasets must be compared at the module level — i.e., you need to identify which module pairs share significantly more genes than expected by chance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-contingency-table-testing))
- **fisher-exact-contingency-testing**: Use when you need to test enrichment of one genomic gene/region set (e.g. histoneHMM differential modification calls) against another (e.g. DESeq-derived DE genes) with a 2x2 contingency table and exact test P-value. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-contingency-testing))
- **fisher-exact-contingency-testing**: Use when reads have been haplotagged/phased (e.g. Margin v2.2 + WhatsHap v1.4) and per-region methylation or 6mA counts per haplotype are needed to call allele-specific CGIs or histone-marked regions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fisher-exact-contingency-testing))
- **fisher-exact-contingency-testing**: Use when testing a categorical association between CMScaller CMS class assignments (e.g., CMS2 vs non-CMS2, CMS1 vs non-CMS1) and a binary phenotype (drug responder status, MSI status, BRAF mutation) across PDX, organoid, or cell line cohorts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-contingency-testing))
- **fisher-exact-driver-enrichment-testing**: Use when validating a CITRUS attention-weight output (attn_wt) by asking if highly attended genes are enriched for known cancer drivers (e.g., IntOGen), or when quantifying overlap/enrichment of two gene/TF sets via Fisher's exact test. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-driver-enrichment-testing))
- **fisher-exact-enrichment-testing**: Use when validating that a ranked list of predicted cis driver genes (e.g., Rank-1 mediators from iEDGE mediation output) is significantly enriched for known driver genes from curated compendia such as COSMIC CGC, UniProt, OMIM, or TUSON. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-enrichment-testing))
- **fisher-exact-enrichment-testing**: Use when two categorical classifications (e.g. dofi vs upfi splicing order) must be compared between a regulated set (DeltaPSI &gt; 0.15, control PSI &lt;= 0.2) and a matched control set to test enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fisher-exact-enrichment-testing))
- **fisher-exact-enrichment-testing**: Use when you have categorical candidate-vs-reference gene assignments across curated loci and need odds ratios with 95% CIs to rank which annotation criteria are enriched for true positives. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fisher-exact-enrichment-testing))
- **fisher-exact-enrichment-testing**: Use when validating a predicted gene/RBP set (e.g., an NMF cluster from CRIT's MATRIX.txt features) against a curated reference list such as gold_regulators.xlsx or POSTAR3/ENCORI CLIP-derived circRNA-binding RBPs via 2x2 overlap tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-enrichment-testing--d446cb87d798774f))
- **fisher-exact-enrichment-testing**: Use when a fitted SSrGE model must be probed for subgroup-specific genotype markers — e.g., after separating normal vs. cancer cell indices, to find eeSNVs/genes significantly enriched in one subgroup (Fisher's exact test p &lt; 0.05). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-enrichment-testing--e215f8c791c4bc9d))
- **fisher-exact-enrichment-testing**: Use when comparing the composition of ligand-receptor sets from CCC resources against annotation terms (pathways, cancer genes, tissue-enriched proteins), or when testing whether top-ranked CCC interactions overlap significantly with active cytokines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-enrichment-testing--14e24cc8fd75fc59))
- **fisher-exact-overrepresentation-testing**: Use when you have a ranked list of TR gene symbols from BIT (e.g., mm10_RUNX1_rank_table.csv) and need to test whether the top-ranked TRs are enriched for GO biological processes relevant to the perturbed or analyzed biological context. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-overrepresentation-testing))
- **fisher-exact-overrepresentation-testing**: Use when you have a list of metabolite annotations exclusive to one method (e.g. METASPACE-ML at FDR 10%) plus a background annotation set, each mapped to HMDB taxonomy subclasses, and need to find which metabolic classes are overrepresented. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fisher-exact-overrepresentation-testing))
- **fisher-exact-pathway-enrichment**: Use when an untargeted LC/MS feature table (m/z, retention time, p-value) must be mapped onto pathway-level biological activity, and metabolite identities are only tentatively matched, so enrichment must tolerate many-to-many m/z-to-metabolite matches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fisher-exact-pathway-enrichment))
- **fisher-exact-pooled-read-testing**: Use when simulating or benchmarking ASAS/differential splicing methods and a simple read-pooled baseline is needed to quantify the power gained by modeling replicates, particularly with injected outlier samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-pooled-read-testing))
- **fisher-exact-proportion-enrichment-testing**: Use when a gene set (e.g., DEGs from rank_genes_groups) must be tested for over-representation within a genomic annotation stratum (e.g., XClone-defined LOH vs non-LOH regions) using proportional counts and an exact test. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-proportion-enrichment-testing))
- **fisher-exact-test**: Use when you must test a 2x2 contingency table with small expected counts: run the exact Fisher test under the fixed-margin null, compute the hypergeometric probability of the observed table, enumerate every table with the same margins, sum the one-tailed and two-tailed exact p-values, compute the odds ratio with the Haldane-Anscombe zero-cell correction, and return the small-expected-count verdict that recommends the exact test over the large-sample approximation. Produces p_obs, p_one_tail, p_two_tail, direction, odds ratio and the verdict. Trigger: fisher exact test, 2x2 contingency table,… ([ashfordeOU/aero-agent-skills](https://github.com/ashfordeOU/aero-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cross-cutting/numerics/fisher-exact-test) · ★8)
- **fisher-exact-test-application**: Use when you have a list of metabolites or lipids with associated p-values (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-test-application))
- **fisher-exact-test-computation**: Use when you have partitioned genomic-metabolomic links into discrete categories (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-test-computation))
- **fisher-exact-test-differential-binding**: Use when you have cell-by-peak AnnData matrices from CC qbed insertions in two sample groups and need condition-biased binding sites plus peak–gene concordance P-values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-test-differential-binding))
- **fisher-exact-test-differential-gene-enrichment**: Use when you have imputed surface protein abundances (cTP-net output) that split one cell type into two subgroups, and you need to test whether the genes differential between those subgroups are enriched, e.g. CD56-high/CD16-low vs CD56-low/CD16-high NK cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-test-differential-gene-enrichment))
- **fisher-exact-test-enrichment**: Use when a per-cluster differential marker list (genes + cluster labels + expression scores such as logFC) needs to be converted into ranked cell-type annotations via overrepresentation testing against a marker database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-test-enrichment))
- **fisher-exact-test-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have a binary gene-cluster absence/presence matrix plus per-genome lifestyle labels and need to find genes significantly enriched in one lifestyle group versus the rest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-test-enrichment-analysis))
- **fisher-exact-test-gene-set-enrichment**: Use when the deCS query input is a list of genes — e.g., a top-N cluster marker gene table with 'cluster' and 'gene' columns or trait-associated genes — rather than a gene expression profile matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-test-gene-set-enrichment))
- **fisher-exact-test-motif-significance**: Use when you have DiNAMO MI-ranked IUPAC motif candidates with per-motif contingency tables (presence/absence counts in signal and control FASTA datasets) and need significance-filtered over-represented motifs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-test-motif-significance))
- **fisher-exact-test-outlier-filtering**: Use when running rROMA/Roma PC1-based gene set activity analysis on normalized expression matrices and needing to protect outlier-gene removal (leave-one-out L1 z-scores) from incorrectly excluding genes whose apparent outlier status is not specific to individual gene sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-test-outlier-filtering))
- **fisher-exact-test-overrepresentation-interpretation**: Use when a candidate motif set (e.g., DiNAMO IUPAC lattice nodes) must be statistically filtered for over-representation in a positive (signal.fa) versus negative (control.fa) dataset with multiple-testing correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-test-overrepresentation-interpretation))
- **fisher-exact-test-statistics**: Use when a user supplies a list of metabolites and/or genes of interest and needs pathway overrepresentation p-values combined across analyte types, with BH and Holm multiple-testing adjustment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fisher-exact-test-statistics))
- **fisher-exact-toxicity-admissibility-testing**: Use when implementing or simulating the TSNP two-stage dose-finding design and you must decide which dose levels are sufficiently safe (not overly toxic) to remain candidates for the OBD. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-exact-toxicity-admissibility-testing))
- **fisher-exact-transcript-enrichment-testing**: Use when per-read or per-site m6A and m5C calls (e.g. CHEUI Model 2 output) are available and the question is whether transcripts co-carried by both modification types are enriched beyond chance, as opposed to within-read co-occurrence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fisher-exact-transcript-enrichment-testing))
- **fisher-geometric-multi-teacher-opd**: Use when consolidating homologous specialist LLMs into one generalist. Multi-teacher on-policy distillation equals Fisher merging to second order; RGBM merge-then-gated-branch procedure. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/other/fisher-geometric-multi-teacher-opd) · ★4)
- **fisher-glasses-tail-certified-metrology**: Tail-certified quantum metrology for quenched sensors — Fisher-zero integrability transition, no-go theorem on averaged Fisher data, universal design laws (safe windows, nondegenerate portfolios, Fisher reserves, Fisher-cut criteria). Activation: quantum metrology, Fisher information, quenched environments, tail certification, NV centers, superconducting qubits, Fisher glass, QFI certification ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/other/fisher-glasses-tail-certified-metrology) · ★4)
- **fisher-growth-15**: A股费雪《怎样选择成长股》15要点机械化：营收CAGR成长跑道、研发费用率、毛利率趋势、连续ROE、盈利含金量、财务保守六维打分，为"闲聊法"调研筛出成长股候选 | A-share Philip Fisher growth-stock scoring: revenue CAGR runway, R&D intensity, margin trend, sustained ROE, cash quality, financial conservatism — a shortlist for scuttlebutt research | A株フィリップ・フィッシャー成長株スコアリング：売上CAGR、研究開発比率、利益率トレンド、持続ROE、キャッシュの質、財務健全性の6次元 ([Serennity007/awesome-stock-quant-skills](https://github.com/Serennity007/awesome-stock-quant-skills/tree/HEAD/my-skills/fisher-growth-15) · ★50)
- **fisher-information-standard-error-estimation**: Use when fitting per-gene Tweedie (Compound Poisson) GLMs to scRNA-seq counts and Wald-type standard errors, p-values, or confidence intervals are needed for regression coefficients after estimating (ϕ, p) by profile likelihood. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-information-standard-error-estimation))
- **fisher-lda-gene-weight-estimation**: Use when a reference scRNA-seq dataset has cluster-specific gene signatures and you need to weight those genes by discriminability before scoring target cells, especially with batch effect or imbalanced library sizes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-lda-gene-weight-estimation))
- **fisher-matrix-forecasting**: Use when you need 1-sigma parameter error forecasts (e.g. sigma(H0), sigma(ombh2)) from a CMB power-spectrum likelihood without running MCMC, or when scanning an experimental design choice (e.g. bin width) against forecast precision. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/fisher-matrix-forecasting))
- **fisher-matrix-parameter-normalisation**: Use when you need a parameter covariance estimate for a differentiable likelihood (e.g. SPT-3G 2018 TT/TE/EE via candl) without finite differences, or when preparing a normalised parameter space for a gradient-based sampler such as BlackJAX NUTS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/fisher-matrix-parameter-normalisation))
- **fisher-memorization-geometry**: Fisher projection de-memorizes networks layer by layer. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/physics-math/fisher-memorization-geometry) · ★4)
- **fisher-method-implementation**: Use when you have a text file of n rows x k columns of independent p-values (one variable per row, e.g. SNPs or genes across tests/traits) and need a combined per-row p-value via Fisher's method. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/fisher-method-implementation))
- **fisher-p-value-combination-testing**: Reference for combining per-molecule statistical p-values into a single pathway-level p-value with Fisher's method when benchmarking pathway-based methods against molecular-level signals in low signal-to-noise multi-omics data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fisher-p-value-combination-testing))
- **fisher-pvalue-combination**: Use when a subpopulation-specific gene ranking must integrate expression evidence from scRNA-seq with enhancer-promoter contact evidence from bulk HiChIP loop counts, e.g. to improve GO enrichment of subpopulations at low sequencing depth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-pvalue-combination))
- **fisher-r-to-z-network-weighting**: Use when a directed/undirected reference network must be re-weighted from case-control high-throughput data (e.g., GSE172114 COVID-19 RNA-seq) so edge costs encode between-group correlation differences prior to Steiner tree or perturbation extraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-r-to-z-network-weighting))
- **fisher-scoring-optimization**: Use when fitting a scDBM or negative-binomial count model to a scRNA-seq UMI count matrix and the per-gene inverse dispersion parameter θ needs to be estimated or periodically refreshed during training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-scoring-optimization))
- **fisher-scriven-analysis**: Use this skill when the user wants guided Fisher–Scriven Argument Analysis applied to real work. It teaches the source-grounded method, halts for observable user work, evaluates each gate, and produces a structured handoff. ([cerredz/Vidbyte-Skills](https://github.com/cerredz/Vidbyte-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fisher-scriven-analysis))
- **fisher-z-confidence-interval-estimation**: Use when you must decide if batch-corrected metabolomics data improves association with a clinical reference (e.g., LC-MS creatinine vs routine clinical creatinine) and need 95% confidence intervals on Pearson r to compare raw vs corrected data versions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fisher-z-confidence-interval-estimation))
- **fisher-z-transformation**: Use when you have an expression matrix split into two sample groups (e.g. tumor vs non-tumor in the Hu-cancer-RNASeq-dataset) and want to identify features whose Pearson correlation with a user-selected target feature differs significantly between groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fisher-z-transformation))
- **fisher-z-transformation-statistics**: Use when you have two condition-specific FPKM expression matrices and a fixed reference network, and need to test whether each edge's Pearson correlation differs significantly between conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fisher-z-transformation-statistics))
- **fisher_mask_search**: Select Transformer heads and filters to prune using diagonal Fisher scores under a FLOPs budget. - **fishers-exact-contingency-testing**: Use when discrete, mutually exclusive counts form a 2x2 table needing an exact P-value — e.g. insertion-carrying vs wild-type reads split by intronic/exonic class at a SCISSOR-flagged junction, or co-altered gene counts between cohorts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fishers-exact-contingency-testing))
- **fishers-exact-enrichment-testing**: Use when comparing binary trait counts (e.g., inverted-Alu-positive vs. Alu-negative BSJs, tissue-specific isoform vs. gene) against a matched control set, and a 2x2 contingency table with scipy.stats.fisher_exact is appropriate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fishers-exact-enrichment-testing))
- **fishers-exact-enrichment-testing**: Use when testing whether a lead variant set (e.g. sQTLs at FDR &lt; 0.05) is enriched in discrete annotation categories relative to matched non-sQTLs or intron sets, and you need odds ratios with Fisher's exact p-values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fishers-exact-enrichment-testing--e8a3d461f6f13fbb))
- **fishers-exact-enrichment-testing**: Use when a single-cell dataset has categorical clone assignments (e.g., mitochondrial clones from vireoSNP at probability &gt;0.8) and independent cell labels (tumor vs non-tumor, CNV subclones) and you need to test enrichment of one clone in one label. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fishers-exact-enrichment-testing--945518df1c660909))
- **fishers-exact-enrichment-testing**: Use when comparing a set of cell-type co-localization pairs detected on reconstructed or predicted spatial coordinates against a reference set of supported co-localizations, to quantify concordance with a p-value. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fishers-exact-enrichment-testing--6704c02b327a91d1))
- **fishers-exact-enrichment-testing**: Use when two sample groups yield sets of candidate regions (e.g., predicted dsRNA regions per sample) and you need to test whether the shared vs group-specific overlap fractions differ significantly between groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fishers-exact-enrichment-testing--7d0e79bbdab75c19))
- **fishers-exact-hypergeometric-enrichment-testing**: Use when a list of differentially abundant metabolites (metabolites of interest) must be tested against a pathway set and background set to obtain per-pathway enrichment p-values and identify significantly enriched pathways. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fishers-exact-hypergeometric-enrichment-testing))
- **fishers-exact-ora-testing**: Use when input is a spatial/single-cell metabolomics matrix with METASPACE MS1 annotations (e.g. FDR &lt;= 0.1, HMDB) and you need ORA while accounting for ions matching multiple isomeric/isobaric candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fishers-exact-ora-testing))
- **fishers-exact-pathway-enrichment**: Use when you have a set of significant metabolites (e.g. absolute fold change &gt;= 1 and adjusted p &lt; 0. - **fishers-exact-proportion-testing**: Use when two discrete cell samples have been clustered (e.g. k=5 ChromSCape clusters) and you need a significance test that the proportion of cells falling in a target cluster differs between the samples.
